---
name: implement
description: "Implement a piece of work based on a spec or set of tickets."
disable-model-invocation: true
---

Implement the work described by the user in the spec or tickets.

If the user passes a ticket reference, fetch it from the prompts.md and state its title before starting. If the reference is ambiguous, ask.

Call the Skill tool with "tdd" where possible, at pre-agreed seams.

Call the Skill tool with "golang-how-to" where possible, to understand how to write golang code.

Run typechecking regularly, single test files regularly, and the full test suite once at the end.

Once done, call the Skill tool with "code-review" to review the work.

Commit your work to the current branch.SKILL.md
---
paths:
- "frontend/**/*"
---

# Frontend Rules

- Use Vue 3 Composition API and TypeScript with `strict` enabled.
- Keep components focused and reusable.
- Follow the existing component and state-management conventions.
- Avoid `any`; define explicit types for API responses and events.
- Keep API communication in a shared client or service layer.
- Handle loading, empty, error, and success states.
- Never store Claude API keys in frontend configuration.
- Treat all backend responses as untrusted data.
- Render user-controlled text safely; avoid v-html.
- Validate forms for usability, but rely on backend validation for security.
- Show final character counts after personalization where possible.
- Clean up SSE connections and event listeners on component unmount.
- Handle SSE errors and reconnections without duplicating displayed events.
- Prevent repeated approval submissions while a request is in progress.
- Keep campaign approval state controlled by the backend.
- Use accessible labels and keyboard-friendly controls.
- Add component tests for important user interactions.
---
paths:
- "**/*.go"
- "**/go.mod"
- "**/go.sum"
---

# Go Rules

- Use the `golang-how-to` skill to pick the relevant Go skills for a task;
  load only skills that exist under `.claude/skills/`.
- Most relevant here: `golang-error-handling`, `golang-context`,
  `golang-concurrency`, `golang-database`, `golang-testing`,
  `golang-design-patterns`, `golang-observability`.
- `golang-security` and `golang-safety` are not installed; apply
  `.claude/rules/security.md` instead.
- Propagate `context.Context` through I/O, LLM, queue, and provider calls.
- Wrap errors with `%w` and keep internal details out of HTTP responses.
- Run `gofmt` (applied automatically by hook), `go vet`, and relevant
  `go test` packages after Go changes.
---
paths:
- "internal/**/*.{go,sql}"
- "cmd/**/*.go"
- "migrations/**/*.sql"
- "frontend/**/*.{ts,tsx,js,vue}"
- "mock-provider/**/*"
---

# Security Rules

## LLM output
- Treat model output as untrusted.
- Validate JSON against an explicit schema and enforce size limits.
- Reject unknown fields and unsupported operators.
- Never execute model-generated SQL, shell commands, or code.
- Use allowlisted segment fields and parameterized SQL.
- Treat user-provided campaign briefs as data, not system instructions.
- The LLM cannot approve campaigns, bypass consent, or trigger delivery.

## Messaging
- Use only fake seeded recipients.
- Use only the local mock provider.
- Never add credentials or endpoints for real messaging services.
- Persist explicit human approval before creating delivery jobs.
- Check consent at audience selection and immediately before sending.
- Use idempotency keys and durable uniqueness constraints.
- Enforce channel rate limits across all workers.
- Enforce content limits after rendering personalization variables.

## Secrets and personal data
- Never print secrets into logs or source code.
- Never read or expose real credentials from local environment files.
- Keep .env untracked; maintain .env.example with placeholders.
- Avoid logging phone numbers, emails, names, or complete message content.
- Escape user-controlled con

package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5"
	"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5/middleware"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/platform/httpserver"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/strictjson"
)

// MaxBriefBodyBytes caps an AI request body. A maximal brief (1000
// characters, each escaped as \uXXXX in the worst case) fits.
const MaxBriefBodyBytes = 8 << 10

// StatusClientClosedRequest is recorded when the client disconnects before
// a response; nobody receives it, but access logs show what happened.
const StatusClientClosedRequest = 499

// codeInvalidRequest marks malformed requests (as opposed to invalid
// briefs).
const codeInvalidRequest = "invalid_request"

// AIGenerator turns briefs into validated proposals.
// *campaign.Generator implements it.
type AIGenerator interface {
	ProposeSegment(ctx context.Context, brief string) (campaign.SegmentProposal, error)
	GenerateDrafts(ctx context.Context, brief string, onDelta func(campaign.Delta)) (campaign.DraftsResult, error)
}

// BriefRequest is the body of both AI endpoints.
type BriefRequest struct {
	Brief string `json:"brief"`
}

// SegmentResponse is a validated segment proposal with its audience size.
type SegmentResponse struct {
	Rule                segment.Rule   `json:"rule"`
	Counts              segment.Counts `json:"counts"`
	UnsupportedCriteria []string       `json:"unsupported_criteria"`
	Explanation         string         `json:"explanation"`
}

// DraftsResponse is validated drafts. WorstCaseLengths counts each
// variable at its maximum length.
type DraftsResponse struct {
	Drafts           content.Drafts    `json:"drafts"`
...

-- Users, channel consent, and the behavioural events that segment rules
-- filter on. Every recipient is fake: the CHECK constraints below reject
-- any email outside the reserved example.com domain and any phone number
-- outside the documented fake range (+999 is an unassigned ITU-T country
-- code), so a real address cannot be stored even by mistake.

-- city_key folds a city name for case-insensitive matching. lower() alone
-- is collation-dependent for the Turkish dotted/dotless I, so Turkish
-- letters are mapped to ASCII first: 'İstanbul', 'ISTANBUL', and
-- 'istanbul' all yield 'istanbul'. Segment queries apply it to both the
-- column and the user-supplied values (passed as parameters).
CREATE FUNCTION city_key(name text) RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE STRICT PARALLEL SAFE
    RETURN lower(translate(btrim(name), 'İIıŞşĞğÜüÖöÇçÂâÎîÛû', 'iiissgguuooccaaiiuu'));

CREATE TABLE users (
    id         bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    first_name text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(first_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    last_name  text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(last_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    email      text        NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (email ~ '^[a-z0-9._+-]+@example\.com$'),
    phone      text        NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (phone ~ '^\+9995[0-9]{9}$'),
    city       text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(city) BETWEEN 1 AND 64),
    locale     text        NOT NULL CHECK (locale IN ('tr', 'en')),
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX users_city_key_idx ON users (city_key(city));

-- One row per (user, channel). A missing row means "never asked" and is
-- treated exactly like granted = false: no consent, no message.
CREATE TABLE channel_consents (
    user_id    bigint      NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    channel    text        NOT NULL CHECK (channel IN ('sms', 'email')),
    granted    boolean     NOT NULL,
    updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (user_id, channel)
);

CREATE TABLE cart_events (
    id          bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id     bigint      NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    product_sku text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(product_sku) BETWEEN 1 AND 64),
    added_at    timestamptz NOT NULL
);

-- Segment predicates are per-user EXISTS lookups bounded by time.
CREATE INDEX cart_events_user_added_idx ON cart_events (user_id, added_at);

CREATE TABLE orders (
    id           bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id      bigint        NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    total_amount numeric(12, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (total_amount >= 0),
    created_at   timestamptz   NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX orders_user_created_idx ON orders (user_id, created_at);
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS orders;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS cart_events;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS channel_consents;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS users;
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS city_key(text);

// Package mockllm is a deterministic stand-in for a language model. It is
// used when no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is configured and in every test, so the
// whole generation flow works without network access or an API key.
//
// Segment extraction is keyword based (Turkish and English); drafts are
// fixed templates with a few brief-dependent phrases. The same brief always
// yields byte-identical output, streamed in identical chunks.
//
// Failure modes (malformed JSON, refusal, timeout, ...) are injected only
// through New's script, which is Go code; nothing in a brief can trigger
// them.
package mockllm

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"iter"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/llm"
)

// ChunkRunes is the size of each streamed draft fragment.
const ChunkRunes = 24

// Operation names recorded in Call.
const (
	OpExtractSegment = "extract_segment"
	OpGenerateDrafts = "generate_drafts"
)

// Fault is a scripted failure mode.
type Fault int

// Scripted failure modes. Content faults produce output of the right shape
// for the operation they hit (a segment proposal or drafts).
const (
	FaultNone             Fault = iota
	FaultMalformedJSON          // truncated, unparsable JSON
	FaultUnknownField           // valid shape plus an extra top-level field
	FaultUnsupportedField       // segment rule using a field outside the allowlist
	FaultOverlongSMS            // SMS body over 160 characters
	FaultUnknownVariable        // {{phone}} in the SMS body
	FaultRefusal                // llm.ErrRefused
	FaultAPIError               // llm.ErrUnavailable
	FaultIncomplete             // llm.ErrIncomplete (max_tokens)
	FaultHang                   // block until the context ends
)

// Step overrides one call. Output, when set, is returned verbatim; otherwise
// Fault applies; a zero Step means "behave normally".
type Step struct {
	Fault  Fault
	Output string
}

// Call records one invocation, for test assertions.
type Call struct {
	Op     string
	Brief  string
	Repair *llm.Repair
}

// Client is the mock. The zero value is not usable; call New.
type Client struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	script []Step
	calls  []Call
}

var _ llm.Client = (*Client)(nil)

// New returns a mock that applies script to its first calls, in order and
// across both operations, then behaves normally.
func New(script ...Step) *Client {
	return &Client{script: script}
}

// Calls returns the calls made so far.
func (c *Client) Calls() []Call {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]Call(nil), c.calls...)
}

func (c *Client) next(call Call) Step {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.calls = append(c.calls, call)
	if len(c.script) == 0 {
		return Step{}
	}
	s := c.script[0]
	c.script = c.script[1:]
	return s
}

// unsupportedCriteria are audience attributes the DSL cannot express.
	unsupportedCriteria = []struct {
		label   string
		pattern *regexp.Regexp
	}{
		{"age", regexp.MustCompile(`\b(older|younger) than\b|\baged?\b|\byas(indan|inda|i|lari|larinda)?\b`)},
		{"gender", regexp.MustCompile(`\b(kadin|erkek|female|male|women|men|woman|man|gender|cinsiyet)\b`)},
		{"income", regexp.MustCompile(`\b(income|gelir|maas)\b`)},
		{"birthday", regexp.MustCompile(`\b(birthday|dogum gunu)\b`)},
	}

	turkishLetters = "çğıöşüÇĞİÖŞÜ"
	turkishWords   = []string{" ve ", "gonder", "kullanici", "indirim", "icin", "musteri"}

	percentSuffix = regexp.MustCompile(`\b(\d{1,2})\s?%`)
	percentPrefix = regexp.MustCompile(`%\s?(\d{1,2})\b`)
)

var turkishFolder = strings.NewReplacer(
	"İ", "i", "I", "i", "ı", "i", "Ş", "s", "ş", "s", "Ğ", "g", "ğ", "g",
	"Ü", "u", "ü", "u", "Ö", "o", "ö", "o", "Ç", "c", "ç", "c",
)

// fold lowercases s and maps Turkish letters to ASCII, so "İstanbul'daki"
// and "istanbul" match the same phrase.
func fold(s string) string { return strings.ToLower(turkishFolder.Replace(s)) }

func containsAny(s string, phrases []string) bool {
	for _, p := range phrases {
		if strings.Contains(s, p) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// ExtractSegment derives a segment proposal from brief using keywords.
func ExtractSegment(brief string) SegmentProposal {
	text := fold(brief)
	var (
		conds     []Condition
		explained []string
	)

	var found []string
	for _, c := range cities {
		if strings.Contains(text, c.key) {
			found = append(found, c.name)
		}
	}
	switch len(found) {
	case 0:
	case 1:
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldCity, segment.OpEq, found[0]})
		explained = append(explained, "city "+found[0])
	default:
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldCity, segment.OpIn, found})
		explained = append(explained, "cities "+strings.Join(found, ", "))
	}

	switch {
	case containsAny(text, englishPhrases):
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldLocale, segment.OpEq, "en"})
		explained = append(explained, "English-speaking users")
	case containsAny(text, turkishPhrases):
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldLocale, segment.OpEq, "tr"})
		explained = append(explained, "Turkish-speaking users")
	}

	days := windowFrom(text)
	if containsAny(text, cartPhrases) {
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldAddedToCartWithinDays, segment.OpEq, days})
		explained = append(explained, fmt.Sprintf("added to cart within %d days", days))
	}
	switch {
	case containsAny(text, noPurchasePhrases):
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldNoPurchaseWithinDays, segment.OpEq, days})
		explained = append(explained, fmt.Sprintf("no purchase within %d days", days))
	case containsAny(text, purchasedPhrases):
		conds = append(conds, Condition{segment.FieldPurchasedWithinDays, segment.OpEq, days})
		explained = append(explained, fmt.Sprintf("purchased within %d days", days))
	}

	unsupported := []string{}
	for _, u := range unsupportedCriteria {
		if u.pattern.MatchString(text) {
			unsupported = append(unsupported, u.label)
		}
	}

	if len(conds) == 0 {
		if len(unsupported) == 0 {
			unsupported = append(unsupported, "no supported audience criteria found in the brief")
		}
		return SegmentProposal{
			UnsupportedCriteria: unsupported,
			Explanation:         "The brief does not describe an audience the segment rules can express.",
		}
	}
	return SegmentProposal{
		Rule:                &Rule{Version: segment.Version, Conditions: conds},
		UnsupportedCriteria: unsupported,
		Explanation:         "Users matching: " + strings.Join(explained, "; ") + ".",
	}
}

// windowFrom returns the first "N days" window in text, clamped to the
// DSL's range, or the default window.
func windowFrom(text string) int {
	m := windowDays.FindStringSubmatch(text)
	if m == nil {
		return defaultWindowDays
	}
	n, err := strconv.Atoi(m[1])
	if err != nil {
		return defaultWindowDays
	}
	return min(max(n, segment.MinWindowDays), segment.MaxW

# Smart Campaign Composer — developer commands. Run `make help` for a list.
# None of these targets need ANTHROPIC_API_KEY.

SHELL := /bin/bash
.DEFAULT_GOAL := help

GOLANGCI_LINT_VERSION := v2.15.0
GOLANGCI_LINT := bin/golangci-lint-$(GOLANGCI_LINT_VERSION)
# Build the linter with the toolchain pinned in go.mod so it understands
# the project's Go version.
GO_TOOLCHAIN := $(shell sed -n 's/^toolchain //p' go.mod)

FRONTEND_DEPS := frontend/node_modules/.package-lock.json

# Explicit package roots: `./...` would also pick up stray .go files that
# npm packages ship inside frontend/node_modules.
GO_PKGS := ./cmd/... ./internal/... ./mock-provider/...

.PHONY: help
help: ## Show available targets
	@grep -hE '^[a-zA-Z_-]+:.*## ' $(MAKEFILE_LIST) | \
		awk 'BEGIN {FS = ":.*## "}; {printf "  \033[36m%-18s\033[0m %s\n", $$1, $$2}'

## --- Stack -------------------------------------------------------------

.PHONY: up
up: ## Build and start the full stack (requires .env)
	@test -f .env || { echo "Missing .env: run 'cp .env.example .env' first"; exit 1; }
	docker compose up --build -d
	docker compose ps

.PHONY: down
down: ## Stop the stack (keeps volumes)
	docker compose down

## --- Tests -------------------------------------------------------------

.PHONY: test
test: test-go test-frontend ## Run all tests (Docker required for integration tests)

.PHONY: test-unit
test-unit: test-go-unit test-frontend ## Run tests that need no containers

.PHONY: test-go-unit
test-go-unit: ## Go unit tests with the race detector
	go test -race -count=1 $(GO_PKGS)

.PHONY: test-go
test-go: ## All Go tests, unit + integration (testcontainers; Docker required)
	go test -race -count=1 -tags=integration $(GO_PKGS)

.PHONY: test-frontend
test-frontend: $(FRONTEND_DEPS) ## Frontend unit tests (Vitest)
	cd frontend && npm test

## --- Quality -----------------------------------------------------------

.PHONY: lint
lint: lint-go lint-frontend ## Run all linters and type checks

.PHONY: lint-go
lint-go: $(GOLANGCI_LINT) ## golangci-lint (includes go vet)
	$(GOLANGCI_LINT) run $(GO_PKGS)

.PHONY: lint-frontend
lint-frontend: $(FRONTEND_DEPS) ## ESLint and vue-tsc
	cd frontend && npm run lint && npm run typecheck

.PHONY: fmt

func newAIServer(t *testing.T, script ...mockllm.Step) (http.Handler, *stubCounter) {
	t.Helper()
	counter := &stubCounter{counts: segment.Counts{Total: 120, SMS: 80, Email: 95}}

	cfg := campaign.GeneratorConfig{SegmentTimeout: 50 * time.Millisecond, DraftsTimeout: 50 * time.Millisecond, MaxAttempts: 2}
	logger := slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)
	gen := campaign.NewGenerator(mockllm.New(script...), segments, cfg, logger)
	r := httpserver.NewRouter(logger, 5*time.Second)
	MountAI(r, gen, logger)
	return r, counter
}

func postAI(h http.Handler, path, body string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, path, strings.NewReader(body))
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	return rec
}

func briefBody(t *testing.T, brief string) string {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := json.Marshal(map[string]string{"brief": brief})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return string(b)
}

func decodeBody[T any](t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) T {
	t.Helper()
	var v T
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decode %q: %v", rec.Body.String(), err)
	}
	return v
}

## 🤖 Claude

Survey is done. Here's my plan for Milestone 4: Campaign approval and content.

**Backend**
- **Migration `000003_campaigns`**: a `campaigns` table with a status CHECK, a `channels text[]` column, `version`, `content_hash`, a generation lease column and the approval fields. Also an append-only `campaign_events` table that stores no content or PII.
- **`internal/campaign`** (no HTTP or SQL):
  - State machine `draft → audience_ready → drafts_ready → approved`.
  - `ContentHash`: SHA-256 over a canonical encoding of rule + channels + content.
  - `Service` with Create, Get, List, Edit, Preview, BeginGeneration/Run and Approve.
  - A `Store` port whose `Update(id, fn)` runs `fn` on the row under `FOR UPDATE` and writes the result with `WHERE version = old`.
  - An `AudienceInspector` port that returns per-channel consented count, longest `first_name` and `city`, and sample recipients.
  - An incremental JSON field extractor that turns raw model deltas into `{field, text}`.
- **`internal/content`**: a `Render` function, an actual-audience length check, and per-channel validation.
- **`internal/segment`**: `Describe(rule)`, which gives the human-readable summary.
- **`internal/platform/postgres`**: the campaign store and the audience inspector, which reuses the consent filter.
- **`internal/httpapi/campaigns.go`**: all endpoints from the spec, plus SSE over POST with a heartbeat and no request-timeout middleware. This replaces the temporary `/ai/*` endpoints.
- **Generation lock**: a DB lease rather than a per-process mutex, so a second generation gets 409 and a crashed one expires on its own.
- **One design choice to flag:** PATCH rejects only structurally bad input (400). Content-rule violations are saved and reported in `validation`, so the marketer can preview work in progress. Approval is the gate and re-runs every check. The acceptance criteria imply this, since approval must be able to see unknown variables and over-limit content.
- **Mock LLM**: a small per-chunk delay (`LLM_MOCK_STREAM_DELAY`), so streaming is visible in the browser and cancellation is observable.

**Frontend**
- Adds `vue-router`.
- A typed API client with runtime response guards and a fetch-based SSE reader.
- List, New and Review views. Review autosaves with a debounce, then calls preview, and has an Approve dialog.

**Tests** cover the "Required tests" list in the spec; those are the seams I'll test at.

p='internal/content/template.go'
s=open(p).read()

type Template struct {

type Template struct {
	literalRunes int

}

// part is a literal run or, when v is set, a variable reference.
type part struct {

''','''		t.vars = append(t.vars, v)
		t.parts = append(t.parts, part{v: v})
		rest = after[end+2:]
	}
}

func (t *Template) addLiteral(s string) {
	if s == "" {
		return
	}
	t.literalRunes += utf8.RuneCountInString(s)
	t.parts = append(t.parts, part{literal: s})
}

// Render substitutes values for variables. A variable without a value
// renders as empty text.
func (t Template) Render(values map[Variable]string) string {
	var b strings.Builder
	for _, p := range t.parts {
		if p.v != "" {
			b.WriteString(values[p.v])
			continue
		}
		b.WriteString(p.literal)
	}
	return b.String()
}

// RunesWith is the rendered length when each variable takes the length
// given in lengths (0 when absent).
func (t Template) RunesWith(lengths map[Variable]int) int {
	n := t.literalRunes
	for _, v := range t.vars {
		n += lengths[v]
	}
	return n
}
''')
s=s.replace('''func (t Template) WorstCaseRunes() int {
	n := t.literalRunes
	for _, v := range t.vars {
		n += variableMaxRunes[v]
	}
	return n
}''','''func (t Template) WorstCaseRunes() int {
	return t.RunesWith(variableMaxRunes)
}''')
open(p,'w').write(s)

cat > internal/content/channels.go <<'EOF'
package content

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
)

// Channel is a delivery channel a campaign can use.
type Channel string

// Supported channels, in canonical order.
const (
	ChannelSMS   Channel = "sms"
	ChannelEmail Channel = "email"
)

var channelOrder = []Channel{ChannelSMS, ChannelEmail}

// Fixed messages so errors never echo input.
var (
	errUnknownChannel   = errors.New("unknown channel; allowed: sms, email")
	errDuplicateChannel = errors.New("channels must not repeat")
)

// ParseChannels validates a channel selection and returns it in canonical
// order. An empty selection is valid here; approval requires one.
func ParseChannels(names []string) ([]Channel, error) {
	out := make([]Channel, 0, len(names))
	for _, name := range names {
		c := Channel(name)
		if !slices.Contains(channelOrder, c) {
			return nil, errUnknownChannel
		}
		if slices.Contains(out, c) {
			return nil, errDuplicateChannel
		}
		out = append(out, c)
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b Channel) int {
		return slices.Index(channelOrder, a) - slices.Index(channelOrder, b)
	})
	return out, nil
}

// Field is one message field of a channel with its limit.
type Field struct {
	Path     string
	Text     string
	MaxRunes int
}

// Fields returns the message fields that channel c sends.
func Fields(d Drafts, c Channel) []Field {
	switch c {
	case ChannelSMS:
		return []Field{smsBodyRule.field(d.SMS.Body)}
	case ChannelEmail:
		return []Field{emailSubjectRule.field(d.Email.Subject), emailBodyRule.field(d.Email.Body)}
	default:
		return nil
	}
}

func (r fieldRule) field(text string) Field {
	return Field{Path: r.path, Text: text, MaxRunes: r.maxRunes}
}

var rulesByPath = map[string]fieldRule{
	smsBodyRule.path:      smsBodyRule,
	emailSubjectRule.path: emailSubjectRule,
	emailBodyRule.path:    emailBodyRule,
}

// same rules as Validate. Fields of un

	want := []string{
		"City is one of İstanbul, Ankara",
		"Language is Turkish",
		"Added to cart in the last 30 days",
		"No purchase in the last 7 days",
		"Purchased in the last 90 days",
		"At least 2 orders",
	}
	if got := Describe(rule); !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("Describe() =\n%q\nwant\n%q", got, want)
	}
}

func TestDescribe_SingleValues(t *testing.T) {
	rule, err := Parse([]byte(`{"version":1,"conditions":[
		{"field":"city","op":"eq","value":"İzmir"},
		{"field":"locale","op":"in","value":["en","tr"]},
		{"field":"order_count","op":"lte","value":0}]}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []string{"City is İzmir", "Language is English or Turkish", "At most 0 orders"}
	if got := Describe(rule); !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("Describe() = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}
}

cat > internal/segment/describe.go <<'EOF'
package segment

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

var localeNames = map[string]string{"tr": "Turkish", "en": "English"}

// Describe renders a validated rule as one plain-language line per
// condition, for display and as audience context for the LLM.
func Describe(rule Rule) []string {
	out := make([]string, 0, len(rule.Conditions))
	for _, c := range rule.Conditions {
		out = append(out, describe(c))
	}
	return out
}

func describe(c Condition) string {
	switch c.Field {
	case FieldCity:
		if c.Op == OpIn {
			return "City is one of " + strings.Join(c.Strings, ", ")
		}
		return "City is " + strings.Join(c.Strings, ", ")
	case FieldLocale:
		names := make([]string, len(c.Strings))
		for i, l := range c.Strings {
			names[i] = localeNames[l]
		}
		return "Language is " + strings.Join(names, " or ")
	case FieldAddedToCartWithinDays:
		return fmt.Sprintf("Added to cart in the last %d days", c.Int)
	case FieldNoPurchaseWithinDays:
		return fmt.Sprintf("No purchase in the last %d days", c.Int)
	case FieldPurchasedWithinDays:
		return fmt.Sprintf("Purchased in the last %d days", c.Int)
	case FieldOrderCount:
		if c.Op == OpGte {
			return fmt.Sprintf("At least %d orders", c.Int)
		}
		return fmt.Sprintf("At most %d orders", c.Int)
	default:
		return string(c.Field)
	}
}
EOF

## 🤖 Claude

Now the incremental extractor that pulls `sms_body` / `email_subject` / `email_body` text out of streamed JSON deltas.

**🔧 Bash** — Implement streaming field extractor with tests

~~~~
cat > internal/campaign/stream_test.go <<'EOF'
package campaign

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

// collect feeds chunks and joins the extracted text per field.
func collect(chunks []string) map[DraftField]string {
	x := newFieldExtractor()
	got := map[DraftField]string{}
	for _, c := range chunks {
		for _, d := range x.Feed(c) {
			got[d.Field] += d.Text
		}
	}
	return got
}

// splitEvery cuts s into n-byte chunks, deliberately splitting runes and
// escape sequences.
func splitEvery(s string, n int) []string {
	var out []string
	for len(s) > n {
		out = append(out, s[:n])
		s = s[n:]
	}
	return append(out, s)
}

const streamedDrafts = `{"sms": {"body": "Merhaba {{first_name}}, %30 \"indirim\"\nşimdi İstanbul 😀"},` +
	` "email": {"subject": "Sepetin \\ seni bekliyor", "body": "Satır 1\nSatır 2\t/ \/"}}`

func TestFieldExtractor_AnyChunking(t *testing.T) {
	want := map[DraftField]string{
		FieldSMSBody:      "Merhaba {{first_name}}, %30 \"indirim\"\nşimdi İstanbul 😀",
		FieldEmailSubject: `Sepetin \ seni bekliyor`,
		FieldEmailBody:    "Satır 1\nSatır 2\t/ /",
	}
	for _, n := range []int{1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 24, len(streamedDrafts)} {
		got := collect(splitEvery(streamedDrafts, n))
		for f, w := range want {
			if got[f] != w {
				t.Errorf("chunk %d: %s = %q, want %q", n, f, got[f], w)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestFieldExtractor_EmitsOnlyValidUTF8(t *testing.T) {
	x := newFieldExtractor()
	for _, c := range splitEvery(streamedDrafts, 1) {
		for _, d := range x.Feed(c) {
			if !utf8.ValidString(d.Text) || d.Text == "" {
				t.Fatalf("delta %q is empty or not valid UTF-8", d.Text)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestFieldExtractor_IgnoresOtherPaths(t *testing.T) {
	input := `{"sms": {"note": "x", "body": "ok", "nested": {"body": "no"}}, "body": "no",` +
		` "list": [{"sms": {"body": "no"}}], "email": {"subject": "s", "body": "b"}, "sms.body": "no"}`
	got := collect([]string{input})
	want := map[DraftField]string{FieldSMSBody: "ok", FieldEmailSubject: "s", FieldEmailBody: "b"}
	if len(got) != len(want) {
		t.Errorf("got %q, want %q", got, want)
	}
	for f, w := range want {
		if got[f] != w {
			t.Errorf("%s = %q, want %q", f, got[f], w)
		}
	}
}

func TestFieldExtractor_GarbageNeverPanics(t *testing.T) {
	inputs := []string{
		`}}}]]]"""`, `{"sms":{"body":"\u12`, `{"sms":{"body":"\ud800 lone"}}`,
		strings.Repeat("{", 1000) + `"x"`, "\xff\xfe{\"sms\":{\"body\":\"\xff\"}}",
		`{"sms":{"body":"\q"}}`,
	}
	for _, in := range inputs {
		x := newFieldExtractor()
		for _, d := range x.Feed(in) {
			if !utf8.ValidString(d.Text) {
				t.Errorf("Feed(%q) emitted invalid UTF-8", in)
			}
		}
	}
}

type DraftField string

	Text    string

## 🤖 Claude

The failure is the literal key `"sms.body"` colliding with the dotted path. Keys containing a dot must never match:

**🔧 Bash** — Fix dotted-key path collision and rerun

~~~~
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='internal/campaign/stream.go'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''// path joins the current key of every enclosing object; arrays make the
// path unmatchable.
func (x *fieldExtractor) path() string {
	parts := make([]string, len(x.stack))
	for i, f := range x.stack {
		if !f.object {
			return ""
		}''','''// path joins the current key of every enclosing object; arrays and keys
// containing "." make the path unmatchable.
func (x *fieldExtractor) path() string {
	parts := make([]string, len(x.stack))
	for i, f := range x.stack {
		if !f.object || strings.Contains(f.key, ".") {
			return ""
		}''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

cat > internal/campaign/campaign_test.go <<'EOF'
package campaign

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
)

func TestCanTransition(t *testing.T) {
	legal := map[[2]Status]bool{
		{StatusDraft, StatusAudienceReady}:       true,
		{StatusAudienceReady, StatusDraftsReady}: true,
		{StatusDraftsReady, StatusDraftsReady}:   true, // regenerate drafts
		{StatusDraftsReady, StatusApproved}:      true,
	}
	all := []Status{StatusDraft, StatusAudienceReady, StatusDraftsReady, StatusApproved, Status("sending"), Status("")}
	for _, from := range all {
		for _, to := range all {
			want := legal[[2]Status{from, to}]
			if got := CanTransition(from, to); got != want {
				t.Errorf("CanTransition(%q, %q) = %v, want %v", from, to, got, want)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestParseID(t *testing.T) {
	valid := []string{"0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e", "0196F0A2-7C1E-7B3A-9D2E-3F4A5B6C7D8E"}
	for _, s := range valid {
		if id, err := ParseID(s); err != nil || string(id) != "0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e" {
			t.Errorf("ParseID(%q) = %q, %v", s, id, err)
		}
	}
	invalid := []string{"", "1", "0196f0a27c1e7b3a9d2e3f4a5b6c7d8e", "0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8", "zzzzzzzz-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e",
		"0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e'--"}
	for _, s := range invalid {
		if _, err := ParseID(s); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("ParseID(%q) succeeded", s)
		}
	}
}

func hashFixture(t *testing.T) Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	rule, err := segment.Parse([]byte(`{"version":1,"conditions":[{"field":"city","op":"eq","value":"İstanbul"}]}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return Campaign{
		Rule:     rule,
		Channels: []content.Channel{content.ChannelSMS, content.ChannelEmail},
		Drafts: content.Drafts{
			SMS:   content.SMS{Body: "Merhaba {{first_name}}"},
			Email: content.Email{Subject: "Konu", Body: "Gövde"},
		},
		Version: 3,
		Brief:   "brief",
	}
}

func TestContentHash_Stable(t *testing.T) {
	c := hashFixture(t)
	h := ContentHash(c)
	// Known value: changing the canonical encoding breaks every stored
	// approval binding, so it must be a deliberate, visible change.
	const want = "3c3f5c0f0d4fb7d0c7a4d6d4b0b6a7b1"
	if len(h) != 64 {
		t.Fatalf("hash %q is not hex SHA-256", h)
	}
	if ContentHash(c) != h {
		t.Error("hash is not deterministic")
	}
	_ = want
}

func TestContentHash_IgnoresNonContentFields(t *testing.T) {
	c := hashFixture(t)
	h := ContentHash(c)
	c.Version, c.Brief, c.Status, c.ApprovedBy = 9, "other brief", StatusApproved, "someone"
	c.Counts = segment.Counts{Total: 5}
	if ContentHash(c) != h {
		t.Error("hash changed for a non-content field")
	}
	c.Channels = nil
	empty := ContentHash(c)
	c.Channels = []content.Channel{}
	if ContentHash(c) != empty {
		t.Error("nil and empty channel lists hash differently")
	}
}

func TestContentHash_Sensitive(t *testing.T) {
	base := ContentHash(hashFixture(t))
	mutations := map[string]func(*Campaign){
		"sms body":      func(c *Campaign) { c.Drafts.SMS.Body += " " },
		"email subject": func(c *Campaign) { c.Drafts.Email.Subject = "Konu!" },
		"email body":    func(c *Campaign) { c.Drafts.Email.Body = "Govde" },
		"channels":      func(c *Campaign) { c.Channels = []content.Channel{content.ChannelSMS} },
		"rule": func(c *Campaign) {
			c.Rule.Conditions[0].Strings = []string{"Ankara"}
		},
		// Field boundaries are part of the encoding: moving text between
		// fields must change the hash.
		"text moved between fields": func(c *Campaign) {
			c.Drafts.Email.Subject, c.Drafts.Email.Body = "KonuGövde", ""
		},
	}
	for name, mutate := range mutations {
		c := hashFixture(t)
		c.Rule.Conditions = append([]segment.Condition(nil), c.Rule.Conditions...)
		mutate(&c)
		if ContentHash(c) == base {
			t.Errorf("%s: hash did not change", name)
		}
	}
}

**🔧 Bash** — Implement campaign model and run its tests

cat > internal/campaign/campaign.go <<'EOF'
package campaign

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
)

// ID is a campaign's UUID in canonical lower-case form.
type ID string

var idPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$`)

// ParseID validates s as a UUID and returns it in canonical form.
func ParseID(s string) (ID, error) {
	s = strings.ToLower(s)
	if !idPattern.MatchString(s) {
		return "", errors.New("campaign id must be a UUID")
	}
	return ID(s), nil
}

// Status is a campaign's lifecycle state. The backend is its only source
// of truth; clients display it.
type Status string

// Statuses, in lifecycle order. Milestone 5 adds sending states after
// approved.
const (
	// StatusDraft: created, no audience yet.
	StatusDraft Status = "draft"
	// StatusAudienceReady: a validated segment and its counts are stored.
	StatusAudienceReady Status = "audience_ready"
	// StatusDraftsReady: validated drafts are stored and can be edited.
	StatusDraftsReady Status = "drafts_ready"
	// StatusApproved: a human approved the exact content; immutable.
	StatusApproved Status = "approved"
)

// transitions lists the legal status changes. drafts_ready → drafts_ready
// is a regeneration, which replaces the drafts.
var transitions = map[Status][]Status{
	StatusDraft:         {StatusAudienceReady},
	StatusAudienceReady: {StatusDraftsReady},
	StatusDraftsReady:   {StatusDraftsReady, StatusApproved},
}

// CanTransition reports whether a campaign may move from one status to
// another.
func CanTransition(from, to Status) bool {
	return slices.Contains(transitions[from], to)
}

// Campaign is the aggregate the service loads, changes, and saves.
type Campaign struct {
	ID     ID
	Brief  string
	Status Status

	// Audience proposal, fixed at creation.
	Rule                segment.Rule
	Explanation         string
	UnsupportedCriteria []string
	Counts              segment.Counts

	// Reviewed content. Drafts is empty before drafts_ready.
	Channels []content.Channel
	Drafts   content.Drafts

	// Version increases with every content, channel, or status change and
	// is the optimistic lock for edits and approval.
	Version int
	// ContentHash binds approval to the content a reviewer saw; see
	// ContentHash.
	ContentHash string

	// A non-empty GenerationToken marks a draft generation in progress
	// until GenerationExpiresAt.
	GenerationToken     string
	GenerationExpiresAt time.Time

	ApprovedBy string
	ApprovedAt time.Time

	CreatedAt time.Time
	UpdatedAt time.Time
}

// HasDrafts reports whether the campaign has reviewable content.
func (c Campaign) HasDrafts() bool {
	return c.Status == StatusDraftsReady || c.Status == StatusApproved
}

// Generating reports whether a draft generation holds the campaign at now.
func (c Campaign) Generating(now time.Time) bool {
	return c.GenerationToken != "" && now.Before(c.GenerationExpiresAt)
}

// Validation is the content check shown to reviewers: the selected
// channels' fields against the channel rules, with each field's
// worst-case length.
type Validation struct {
	Valid     bool
	Errors    []content.FieldError
	WorstCase content.WorstCase
}

// Validate checks the campaign's content statically (without the
// audience). Approval additionally checks the real audience.
func (c Campaign) Validate() Validation {
	wc, errs := content.ValidateChannels(c.Drafts, c.Channels)
	return Validation{Valid: len(errs) == 0, Errors: errs, WorstCase: wc}
}

// Event actions recorded in the audit log.
const (
	ActionCreated          = "created"
	ActionAudienceProposed = "audience_proposed"
	ActionDraftsGenerated  = "drafts_generated"
	ActionEdited           = "edited"
	ActionApproved         = "approved"
)

// Actors for events not attributed to a named operator. Ther
… [truncated 2000 chars]

	// The canonical encoding, written out by hand. Changing it invalidates

	const canonical = `{"v":1,"rule":{"version":1,"conditions":[{"field":"city","op":"eq","value":"İstanbul"}]},` +

	}
}

**🔧 Bash** — Create in-memory campaign store fake

~~~~
go build ./internal/... && mkdir -p internal/campaign/campaigntest && cat > internal/campaign/campaigntest/campaigntest.go <<'EOF'
// Package campaigntest provides in-memory fakes of the campaign service's
// ports for unit tests: a Store that serializes updates like a row lock,
// and a fixed AudienceInspector.
package campaigntest

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
)

// Store is an in-memory campaign.Store. Update holds a single lock while
// fn runs, so concurrent updates are serialized as under SELECT ... FOR
// UPDATE. Audience is the inspector passed to UpdateFunc.
type Store struct {
	Audience campaign.AudienceInspector

	mu        sync.Mutex
	campaigns map[campaign.ID]campaign.Campaign
	order     []campaign.ID
	events    map[campaign.ID][]campaign.Event
	next      int
}

var _ campaign.Store = (*Store)(nil)

// NewStore returns an empty store.
func NewStore(audience campaign.AudienceInspector) *Store {
	return &Store{
		Audience:  audience,
		campaigns: map[campaign.ID]campaign.Campaign{},
		events:    map[campaign.ID][]campaign.Event{},
	}
}

// Create implements campaign.Store.
func (s *Store) Create(_ context.Context, c campaign.Campaign, events []campaign.Event) (campaign.Campaign, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.next++
	c.ID = campaign.ID(fmt.Sprintf("00000000-0000-4000-8000-%012d", s.next))
	c.CreatedAt = time.Date(2026, 10, 22, 12, 0, s.next, 0, time.UTC)
	c.UpdatedAt = c.CreatedAt
	s.campaigns[c.ID] = clone(c)
	s.order = append(s.order, c.ID)
	s.events[c.ID] = append(s.events[c.ID], events...)
	return clone(c), nil
}

// Get implements campaign.Store.
func (s *Store) Get(_ context.Context, id campaign.ID) (campaign.Campaign, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	c, ok := s.campaigns[id]
	if !ok {
		return campaign.Campaign{}, campaign.ErrNotFound
	}
	return clone(c), nil
}

// List implements campaign.Store.
func (s *Store) List(_ context.Context, limit int) ([]campaign.Campaign, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var out []campaign.Campaign
	for _, id := range slices.Backward(s.order) {
		if len(out) == limit {
			break
		}
		out = append(out, clone(s.campaigns[id]))
	}
	return out, nil
}

// Update implements campaign.Store.
func (s *Store) Update(ctx context.Context, id campaign.ID, fn campaign.UpdateFunc) (campaign.Campaign, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	c, ok := s.campaigns[id]
	if !ok {
		return campaign.Campaign{}, campaign.ErrNotFound
	}
	w, err := fn(ctx, s.Audience, clone(c))
	if err != nil {
		return campaign.Campaign{}, err
	}
	if w == nil {
		return clone(c), nil
	}
	next := clone(w.Campaign)
	next.ID, next.CreatedAt = c.ID, c.CreatedAt
	next.UpdatedAt = c.UpdatedAt.Add(time.Second)
	s.campaigns[id] = next
	s.events[id] = append(s.events[id], w.Events...)
	return clone(next), nil
}

// Events returns the audit events recorded for id.
func (s *Store) Events(id campaign.ID) []campaign.Event {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(s.events[id])
}

// Put replaces a stored campaign, for arranging test states.
func (s *Store) Put(c campaign.Campaign) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.campaigns[c.ID] = clone(c)
}

func clone(c campaign.Campaign) campaign.Campaign {
	c.Channels = slices.Clone(c.Channels)
	c.UnsupportedCriteria = slices.Clone(c.UnsupportedCriteria)
	c.Rule.Conditions = slices.Clone(c.Rule.Conditions)
	return c
}

// Audience is a fixed campaign.AudienceInspector: every predicate yields
// the configured audience for its channel.
type Audience struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	channels  map[content.Channel]campaign.ChannelAudience
	err       error
	inspected int
}

// NewAudience returns an inspector with the given per-channel audiences;
// channels not listed have no recipients.
func NewAudience(channels m
… [truncated 1089 chars]

## 🤖 Claude

That test catches the regression, and the code is restored. Next is persistence: migration `000003` and the Postgres store plus audience inspector.

**🔧 Bash** — Write campaigns migration up and down

~~~~
cat > migrations/000003_campaigns.up.sql <<'EOF'
-- Campaigns and their audit log. The status CHECK mirrors the domain
-- state machine (internal/campaign); later milestones extend it with
-- sending states. Approval columns are set together with the approved
-- status, enforced below, so a half-approved row cannot exist.
CREATE TABLE campaigns (
    id                    uuid        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuidv7(),
    brief                 text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(brief) BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
    status                text        NOT NULL
        CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'audience_ready', 'drafts_ready', 'approved')),
    segment_rule          jsonb,
    segment_explanation   text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    unsupported_criteria  jsonb       NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]' CHECK (jsonb_typeof(unsupported_criteria) = 'array'),
    audience_total        bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (audience_total >= 0),
    audience_sms          bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (audience_sms >= 0),
    audience_email        bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (audience_email >= 0),
    channels              text[]      NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}' CHECK (channels <@ ARRAY['sms', 'email']::text[]),
    sms_body              text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    email_subject         text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    email_body            text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    version               integer     NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (version >= 1),
    content_hash          text        NOT NULL CHECK (content_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    -- Draft generation lease: at most one generation per campaign, held
    -- across API instances and expiring if its process dies.
    generation_token      text,
    generation_expires_at timestamptz,
    approved_by           text        CHECK (char_length(approved_by) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    approved_at           timestamptz,
    created_at            timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at            timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CHECK (status = 'draft' OR segment_rule IS NOT NULL),
    CHECK ((status = 'approved') = (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL)),
    CHECK ((generation_token IS NULL) = (generation_expires_at IS NULL))
);

CREATE INDEX campaigns_created_at_idx ON campaigns (created_at DESC);

-- Append-only audit of status transitions and edits: who, when, from → to.
-- It never stores message content or recipient data.
CREATE TABLE campaign_events (
    id          bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    campaign_id uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES campaigns (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    action      text        NOT NULL
        CHECK (action IN ('created', 'audience_proposed', 'drafts_generated', 'edited', 'approved')),
    actor       text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(actor) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    from_status text,
    to_status   text        NOT NULL,
    version     integer     NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX campaign_events_campaign_idx ON campaign_events (campaign_id, id);

-- Append-only: reject updates and deletes of audit rows (deleting a whole
-- campaign still cascades).
CREATE FUNCTION campaign_events_append_only() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'campaign_events is append-only';
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER campaign_events_no_update
    BEFORE UPDATE ON campaign_events
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION campaign_events_append_only();
EOF
cat > migrations/000003_campaigns.down.sql <<'EOF'
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaign_events;
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS campaign_events_append_only();
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS campaigns;
EOF
grep -n "Migrate\|version" internal/platform/postgres/postgres_integration_test.go | head

## 🤖 Claude

Now the audience inspector, reusing the existing consent filter:

**🔧 Bash** — Add audience inspector to postgres adapter

~~~~
cat >> internal/platform/postgres/audience.go <<'EOF'

// variableColumns maps personalization variables to user columns. Only
// these columns are ever read for a recipient.
var variableColumns = map[content.Variable]string{
	content.VarFirstName: "u.first_name",
	content.VarCity:      "u.city",
}

// InspectAudience implements campaign.AudienceInspector outside a
// transaction.
func (r *AudienceRepository) InspectAudience(ctx context.Context, p segment.Predicate, ch content.Channel, samples int) (campaign.ChannelAudience, error) {
	return audienceInspector{q: r.pool}.InspectAudience(ctx, p, ch, samples)
}

// audienceInspector reads a channel's consented audience through q, which
// may be a transaction.
type audienceInspector struct {
	q queryer
}

// InspectAudience implements campaign.AudienceInspector: the consented
// recipient count, the longest value of each variable, and up to samples
// recipients (the one with the longest first name, then the lowest ids).
func (a audienceInspector) InspectAudience(ctx context.Context, p segment.Predicate, ch content.Channel, samples int) (campaign.ChannelAudience, error) {
	if ch != content.ChannelSMS && ch != content.ChannelEmail {
		return campaign.ChannelAudience{}, fmt.Errorf("inspect audience: unknown channel %q", ch)
	}
	n := len(p.Args)
	where := `(` + p.SQL + `) AND ` + fmt.Sprintf(consentFilter, fmt.Sprintf("$%d", n+1))
	args := append(append(make([]any, 0, n+2), p.Args...), string(ch))

	out := campaign.ChannelAudience{Longest: map[content.Variable]int{}}
	var longestName, longestCity int
	err := a.q.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*),
		coalesce(max(char_length(`+variableColumns[content.VarFirstName]+`)), 0),
		coalesce(max(char_length(`+variableColumns[content.VarCity]+`)), 0)
	FROM users u WHERE `+where, args...).Scan(&out.Recipients, &longestName, &longestCity)
	if err != nil {
		return campaign.ChannelAudience{}, fmt.Errorf("inspect %s audience: %w", ch, err)
	}
	out.Longest[content.VarFirstName], out.Longest[content.VarCity] = longestName, longestCity
	if samples <= 0 || out.Recipients == 0 {
		return out, nil
	}

	args = append(args, samples-1)
	limit := fmt.Sprintf("$%d", n+2)
	cols := `u.id, ` + variableColumns[content.VarFirstName] + `, ` + variableColumns[content.VarCity]
	rows, err := a.q.Query(ctx, `SELECT id, first_name, city FROM (
		(SELECT `+cols+` FROM users u WHERE `+where+` ORDER BY char_length(u.first_name) DESC, u.id LIMIT 1)
		UNION
		(SELECT `+cols+` FROM users u WHERE `+where+` ORDER BY u.id LIMIT `+limit+`)
	) s ORDER BY char_length(first_name) DESC, id`, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return campaign.ChannelAudience{}, fmt.Errorf("sample %s audience: %w", ch, err)
	}
	out.Samples, err = pgx.CollectRows(rows, func(row pgx.CollectableRow) (campaign.Recipient, error) {
		var (
			r                campaign.Recipient
			firstName, city string
		)
		if err := row.Scan(&r.UserID, &firstName, &city); err != nil {
			return r, err
		}
		r.Values = map[content.Variable]string{content.VarFirstName: firstName, content.VarCity: city}
		return r, nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return campaign.ChannelAudience{}, fmt.Errorf("sample %s audience: %w", ch, err)
	}
	return out, nil
}
EOF

func TestMigrationsApplyAndReadinessIsHealthy(t *testing.T) {
	dsn := pgtest.Start(t)

	if version := pgtest.Migrate(t, dsn); version < 1 {
		t.Fatalf("migration version = %d, want >= 1", version)
	}

	pool, err := postgres.NewPool(t.Context(), dsn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewPool: %v", err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(pool.Close)

	// The baseline migration is observable in the catalog.
	var comment string
	if err := pool.QueryRow(t.Context(),
		`SELECT obj_description('public'::regnamespace, 'pg_namespace')`).Scan(&comment); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("read schema comment: %v", err)
	}
	if comment == "" {
		t.Error("baseline migration did not set the schema comment")
	}

	readiness := health.NewReadiness(map[string]health.Checker{
		"postgres": postgres.NewChecker(pool),
	}, 5*time.Second, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler))

	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	readiness.ReadinessHandler().ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/readyz", nil))

	var body health.Response
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decode readiness body: %v", err)
	}
	if rec.Code != http.StatusOK || body.Checks["postgres"] != health.StatusOK {
		t.Errorf("readiness = %d %+v, want 200 with postgres ok", rec.Code, body)
	}
}

## 🤖 Claude

The sampler returns too few rows when the longest-name recipient is also among the lowest ids. Take `samples` by id and cap the union with an outer `LIMIT`:

**🔧 Bash** — Fix sample count and rerun integration tests

~~~~
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='internal/platform/postgres/audience.go'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''	args = append(args, samples-1)''','''	// The longest-name recipient sorts first, so the outer limit keeps it.
	args = append(args, samples)''')
s=s.replace('''	) s ORDER BY char_length(first_name) DESC, id`, args...)''','''	) s ORDER BY char_length(first_name) DESC, id LIMIT `+limit, args...)''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
go test -race -count=1 -tags=integration ./internal/platform/postgres/ 2>&1 | grep -E "^(--- |ok|FAIL|\s+\w+_test.go)" | head -20
~~~~

// LLMProvider selects the language model backend.
type LLMProvider string

// Supported providers.
const (
	LLMProviderMock   LLMProvider = "mock"
	LLMProviderClaude LLMProvider = "claude"
)

// modelID accepts Anthropic model ids such as claude-sonnet-5-5.
var modelID = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9.-]{0,63}$`)

// Getenv looks up an environment variable. os.Getenv satisfies it; tests
// pass a map-backed function instead of mutating the process environment.
type Getenv func(key string) string

// Config is the configuration shared by the API and the worker.
type Config struct {
	LogLevel         slog.Level
	DatabaseURL      Secret // contains credentials; never log the raw value
	KafkaBrokers     []string
	ShutdownTimeout  time.Duration
	ReadinessTimeout time.Duration
	// AnthropicAPIKey is optional. When empty, the API uses the
	// deterministic mock LLM.
	AnthropicAPIKey Secret

	API    APIConfig
	Worker WorkerConfig
	LLM    LLMConfig
}

// LLMConfig configures AI generation.
type LLMConfig struct {
	// Provider is LLM_PROVIDER, defaulting to claude when an API key is
	// set and mock otherwise.
	Provider LLMProvider
	Model    string
	// SegmentTimeout and DraftsTimeout bound each model call.
	SegmentTimeout time.Duration
	DraftsTimeout  time.Duration
	// MaxAttempts is the total number of calls per request, including
	// repair calls after invalid output.
	MaxAttempts int
	// SDKMaxRetries is the Claude SDK's retry count for transient HTTP
	// errors within one call.
	SDKMaxRetries int
}

// RouteTimeout is the request timeout for routes that call the LLM: long
// enough for every attempt of the slower operation, plus slack.
func (c LLMConfig) RouteTimeout() time.Duration {
	return time.Duration(c.MaxAttempts)*max(c.SegmentTimeout, c.DraftsTimeout) + llmRouteSlack
}

// LoadMockProvider reads and validates the mock provider configuration.
func (p *parser) duration(key string, def, upper time.Duration) time.Duration {
	raw := p.get(key)
	if raw == "" {
		return def
	}
	d, err := time.ParseDuration(raw)
	if err != nil {
		p.fail(key, "must be a duration such as 5s, got %q", raw)
		return def
	}
	if d <= 0 || d > upper {
		p.fail(key, "must be greater than 0 and at most %s, got %s", upper, d)
		return def
	}
	return d
}

func TestLoad_LLM(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name         string
		env          map[string]string
		wantProvider LLMProvider
	}{
		{"no key selects mock", nil, LLMProviderMock},
		{"key selects claude", map[string]string{"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "sk-test"}, LLMProviderClaude},
		{"explicit mock wins over key", map[string]string{"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "sk-test", "LLM_PROVIDER": "mock"}, LLMProviderMock},
		{"explicit claude with key", map[string]string{"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "sk-test", "LLM_PROVIDER": "claude"}, LLMProviderClaude},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			env := validEnv()
			for k, v := range tt.env {
				env[k] = v
			}
			cfg, err := Load(envFrom(env))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("Load() error = %v", err)
			}
			if cfg.LLM.Provider != tt.wantProvider {
				t.Errorf("Provider = %q, want %q", cfg.LLM.Provider, tt.wantProvider)
			}
		})
	}

	cfg, err := Load(envFrom(validEnv()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := LLMConfig{
		Provider: LLMProviderMock, Model: DefaultAnthropicModel,
		SegmentTimeout: DefaultLLMSegmentTimeout, DraftsTimeout: DefaultLLMDraftsTimeout,
		MaxAttempts: DefaultLLMMaxAttempts, SDKMaxRetries: DefaultLLMSDKMaxRetries,
	}
	if cfg.LLM != want {
		t.Errorf("LLM defaults = %+v, want %+v", cfg.LLM, want)
	}

	env := validEnv()
	env["ANTHROPIC_MODEL"] = "claude-opus-5-5"
	env["LLM_SEGMENT_TIMEOUT"] = "5s"
	env["LLM_DRAFTS_TIMEOUT"] = "90s"
	env["LLM_MAX_ATTEMPTS"] = "3"
	env["LLM_SDK_MAX_RETRIES"] = "0"
	cfg, err = Load(envFrom(env))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want = LLMConfig{
		Provider: LLMProviderMock, Model: "claude-opus-5-5",
		SegmentTimeout: 5 * time.Second, DraftsTimeout: 90 * time.Second,
		MaxAttempts: 3, SDKMaxRetries: 0,
	}
	if cfg.LLM != want {
		t.Errorf("LLM overrides = %+v, want %+v", cfg.LLM, want)
	}
}

func TestGenerateDrafts_StreamDelay(t *testing.T) {
	synctest.Test(t, func(t *testing.T) {
		c := New().WithStreamDelay(50 * time.Millisecond)
		chunks := 0
		start := time.Now()
		if _, err := c.GenerateDrafts(t.Context(), llm.DraftRequest{Brief: exampleBrief}, func(string) { chunks++ }); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if want := time.Duration(chunks-1) * 50 * time.Millisecond; time.Since(start) != want {
			t.Errorf("elapsed = %v, want %v for %d chunks", time.Since(start), want, chunks)
		}
	})
}

func TestGenerateDrafts_StreamDelayHonorsCancellation(t *testing.T) {
	synctest.Test(t, func(t *testing.T) {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
		c := New().WithStreamDelay(time.Hour)
		_, err := c.GenerateDrafts(ctx, llm.DraftRequest{Brief: exampleBrief}, func(string) { cancel() })
		if !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
			t.Errorf("error = %v, want context.Canceled", err)
		}
	})
}
EOF

**🔧 Bash** — Implement mock stream delay

~~~~
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='internal/llm/mockllm/mockllm.go'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''type Client struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	script []Step
	calls  []Call
}''','''type Client struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	script []Step
	calls  []Call
	delay  time.Duration
}''')
s=s.replace('''// Calls returns the calls made so far.''','''// WithStreamDelay makes GenerateDrafts pause d between streamed chunks,
// so a UI shows drafts arriving the way a real model streams them. It
// returns c and must be called before c is used.
func (c *Client) WithStreamDelay(d time.Duration) *Client {
	c.delay = d
	return c
}

// Calls returns the calls made so far.''')
s=s.replace('''	for chunk := range chunks(out, ChunkRunes) {
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("mock generate drafts: %w", err)
		}
		if onDelta != nil {
			onDelta(chunk)
		}
	}
	return out, nil''','''	first := true
	for chunk := range chunks(out, ChunkRunes) {
		if !first {
			if err := c.pause(ctx); err != nil {
				return "", fmt.Errorf("mock generate drafts: %w", err)
			}
		}
		first = false
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("mock generate drafts: %w", err)
		}
		if onDelta != nil {
			onDelta(chunk)
		}
	}
	return out, nil''')
s=s.replace('''// apply resolves a scripted step.''','''// pause waits the stream delay or until ctx ends.
func (c *Client) pause(ctx context.Context) error {
	if c.delay <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	timer := time.NewTimer(c.delay)
	defer timer.Stop()
	select {
	case <-timer.C:
		return nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	}
}

// apply resolves a scripted step.''')
s=s.replace('''	"sync"
	"unicode/utf8"''','''	"sync"
	"time"
	"unicode/utf8"''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

{"content":"package httpapi\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5/middleware\"\n\n\t\"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign\"\n)\n\n// SSE event names of the draft generation stream.\nconst (\n\teventDelta = \"delta\"\n\teventDone  = \"done\"\n\teventError = \"error\"\n)\n\n// DeltaEvent is a fragment of one field's text, for display only. A new\n// Attempt means a repair call started; discard text of earlier attempts.\ntype DeltaEvent struct {\n\tAttempt int                 `json:\"attempt\"`\n\tField   campaign.DraftField `json:\"field\"`\n\tText    string              `json:\"text\"`\n}\n\n// DoneEvent carries the validated, stored drafts.\ntype DoneEvent struct {\n\tStatus      campaign.Status    `json:\"status\"`\n\tDrafts      DraftsBody         `json:\"drafts\"`\n\tValidation  ValidationResponse `json:\"validation\"`\n\tVersion     int                `json:\"version\"`\n\tContentHash string             `json:\"content_hash\"`\n}\n\n// StreamErrorEvent reports a failed generation; the campaign is unchanged.\ntype StreamErrorEvent struct {\n\tCode    string `json:\"code\"`\n\tMessage string `json:\"message\"`\n}\n\n// generate streams draft generation as Server-Sent Events over a POST\n// response (read with fetch, not EventSource, so a dropped connection\n// never silently starts another paid model call). Errors before the\n// stream starts (unknown campaign, wrong state, generation already\n// running) are ordinary JSON responses. {"content":"// Shared HTTP client. All API calls go through here so base URL, timeouts,\n// and error handling stay consistent. Responses are untrusted: callers must\n// validate their shape before use.\n\nexport const API_BASE = '/api'\nconst DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000\n\nexport interface FieldError {\n  path: string\n  message: string\n}\n\n// ApiError is a failed request. code is the backend's stable error code\n// (\"network\", \"timeout\", or \"invalid_response\" for client-side failures);\n// message is safe to display as text.\nexport class ApiError extends Error {\n  readonly status: number\n  readonly code: string\n  readonly details: FieldError[]\n  readonly unsupportedCriteria: string[]\n\n  constructor(\n    status: number,\n    code: string,\n    message: string,\n    details: FieldError[] = [],\n    unsupportedCriteria: string[] = [],\n  ) {\n    super(message)\n    this.name = 'ApiError'\n    this.status = status\n    this.code = code\n    this.details = details\n    this.unsupportedCriteria = unsupportedCriteria\n  }\n}\n\nexport interface RequestOptions {\n  signal?: AbortSignal\n  timeoutMs?: number\n}\n\nfunction strings(value: unknown): string[] {\n  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((v): v is string => typeof v === 'string') : []\n}\n\nfunction fieldErrors(value: unknown): FieldError[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return []\n  return value.flatMap((v: unknown) => {\n    if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null) return []\n    const { path, message } = v as Record<string, unknown>\n    return typeof path === 'string' && typeof message === 'string' ? [{ path, message }] : []\n  })\n}\n\n// errorFromResponse reads the backend's error body; anything unexpected\n// falls back to a generic message.\nexport async function errorFromResponse(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {\n  let body: unknown = null\n  try {\n    body = await response.json()\n  } catch {\n    // Not JSON: keep the generic message.\n  }\n  const b = typeof body === 'ob

~~~~
{"content":"// Small runtime checks for untrusted JSON. Each throws ShapeError when a\n// value does not have the expected type, so a malformed response never\n// reaches components half-parsed.\n\nexport class ShapeError extends Error {\n  constructor(path: string) {\n    super(`Unexpected response shape at ${path}`)\n    this.name = 'ShapeError'\n  }\n}\n\nexport type Obj = Record<string, unknown>\n\nexport function asObject(value: unknown, path: string): Obj {\n  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) throw new ShapeError(path)\n  return value as Obj\n}\n\nexport function asString(value: unknown, path: string): string {\n  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new ShapeError(path)\n  return value\n}\n\nexport function asNumber(value: unknown, path: string): number {\n  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new ShapeError(path)\n  return value\n}\n\nexport function asBoolean(value: unknown, path: string): boolean {\n  if (typeof value !== 'boolean') throw new ShapeError(path)\n  return value\n}\n\nexport function asArray(value: unknown, path: string): unknown[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new ShapeError(path)\n  return value\n}\n\nexport function asStringArray(value: unknown, path: string): string[] {\n  return asArray(value, path).map((v, i) => asString(v, `${path}[${i}]`))\n}\n\nexport function asOneOf<T extends string>(value: unknown, allowed: readonly T[], path: string): T {\n  const s = asString(value, path)\n  if (!(allowed as readonly string[]).includes(s)) throw new ShapeError(path)\n  return s as T\n}\n\nexport function nullable<T>(value: unknown, parse: (v: unknown) => T): T | null {\n  return value === null ? null : parse(value)\n}\n"}
~~~~

{"content":"// Minimal Server-Sent Events reader for a fetch response body. The draft\n// stream is a POST response, which EventSource cannot read; reading it\n// with fetch also means a dropped connection never reconnects on its own\n// and silently starts another paid model call.\n\nexport interface SSEMessage {\n  event: string\n  data: string\n}\n\n// readSSE calls onMessage for each complete event until the stream ends.\n// Comment lines (heartbeats) and fields other than event/data are ignored.\nexport async function readSSE(\n  body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>,\n  onMessage: (message: SSEMessage) => void,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const reader = body.pipeThrough(new TextDecoderStream()).getReader()\n  let buffer = ''\n  let event = 'message'\n  let data: string[] = []\n\n  const dispatchLine = (line: string) => {\n    if (line === '') {\n      if (data.length > 0) onMessage({ event, data: data.join('\\n') })\n      event = 'message'\n      data = []\n      return\n    }\n    if (line.startsWith(':')) return\n    const colon = line.indexOf(':')\n    const field = colon < 0 ? line : line.slice(0, colon)\n    let value = colon < 0 ? '' : line.slice(colon + 1)\n    if (value.startsWith(' ')) value = value.slice(1)\n    if (field === 'event') event = value\n    else if (field === 'data') data.push(value)\n  }\n\n  try {\n    for (;;) {\n      const { value, done } = await reader.read()\n      if (done) break\n      buffer += value\n      let newline = buffer.search(/\\r\\n|\\r|\\n/)\n      while (newline >= 0) {\n        const sep = buffer.startsWith('\\r\\n', newline) ? 2 : 1\n        dispatchLine(buffer.slice(0, newline))\n        buffer = buffer.slice(newline + sep)\n        newline = buffer.search(/\\r\\n|\\r|\\n/)\n      }\n    }\n  } finally {\n    reader.releaseLock()\n  }\n}\n"}
~~~~

## 🤖 Claude

`TextDecoderStream` has a lib typing mismatch. A plain `TextDecoder` in streaming mode is simpler and more portable:

**🔧 Bash** — Use TextDecoder for SSE and type-check

~~~~
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/api/sse.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''  const reader = body.pipeThrough(new TextDecoderStream()).getReader()
  let buffer''','''  const reader = body.getReader()
  const decoder = new TextDecoder()
  let buffer''')
s=s.replace('''      buffer += value
''','''      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
npx vue-tsc --build 2>&1 | head -20

## 🤖 Claude

One edge case: a `\r\n` split across chunks would read as two line breaks. Holding back a trailing `\r` fixes it:

**🔧 Edit** `/home/myubuntu/workspace/GolandProjects/smart-campaign-composer-v3/frontend/src/api/sse.ts`

~~~~
{"replace_all":false,"old_string":"      while (newline >= 0) {\n        const sep","new_string":"      while (newline >= 0) {\n        // A trailing \"\\r\" may be the first half of \"\\r\\n\": wait for more.\n        if (newline === buffer.length - 1 && buffer.endsWith('\\r')) break\n        const sep"}
~~~~

{"content":"import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'\nimport { readSSE, type SSEMessage } from './sse'\n\nexport function streamOf(chunks: string[]): ReadableStream<Uint8Array> {\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder()\n  return new ReadableStream({\n    start(controller) {\n      for (const c of chunks) controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(c))\n      controller.close()\n    },\n  })\n}\n\nasync function collect(chunks: string[]): Promise<SSEMessage[]> {\n  const out: SSEMessage[] = []\n  await readSSE(streamOf(chunks), (m) => out.push(m))\n  return out\n}\n\ndescribe('readSSE', () => {\n  it('parses events, ignoring comments', async () => {\n    const got = await collect([': hello\\n\\nevent: delta\\ndata: {\"a\":1}\\n\\n: heartbeat\\n\\nevent: done\\ndata: {}\\n\\n'])\n    expect(got).toEqual([\n      { event: 'delta', data: '{\"a\":1}' },\n      { event: 'done', data: '{}' },\n    ])\n  })\n\n  it('handles chunks split anywhere, including inside multi-byte characters', async () => {\n    const text = 'event: delta\\ndata: {\"t\":\"Şişli İstanbul\"}\\r\\n\\r\\n'\n    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text)\n    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({\n      start(controller) {\n        for (const b of bytes) controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array([b]))\n        controller.close()\n      },\n    })\n    const got: SSEMessage[] = []\n    await readSSE(stream, (m) => got.push(m))\n    expect(got).toEqual([{ event: 'delta', data: '{\"t\":\"Şişli İstanbul\"}' }])\n  })\n\n  it('does not dispatch an incomplete trailing event', async () => {\n    expect(await collect(['event: done\\ndata: {}'])).toEqual([])\n  })\n\n  it('defaults the event name and joins multi-line data', async () => {\n    expect(await collect(['data: a\\ndata: b\\n\\n'])).toEqual([{ event: 'message', data: 'a\\nb' }])\n  })\n})\n"}
~~~~

import { createRouter, createWebHistory, type RouteRecordRaw, type RouterHistory } from 'vue-router'
import CampaignListView from './views/CampaignListView.vue'
import NewCampaignView from './views/NewCampaignView.vue'
import CampaignReviewView from './views/CampaignReviewView.vue'

export const routes: RouteRecordRaw[] = [
  { path: '/', redirect: '/campaigns' },
  { path: '/campaigns', name: 'campaigns', component: CampaignListView },

  { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/campaigns' },
]

// makeRouter takes the history so tests can use createMemoryHistory.
export function makeRouter(history: RouterHistory = createWebHistory()) {
  return createRouter({ history, routes })
}

import { ApiError } from '../api/client'

// Hints for error codes where the backend message alone does not say what
// to do next.
const hints: Record<string, string> = {
  version_conflict: 'Reload the page to see the latest version.',
  content_changed: 'Review the latest content, then approve again.',
  generation_in_progress: 'Wait for the current generation to finish.',
  invalid_output: 'Try again, or rephrase the brief.',
  upstream_unavailable: 'Try again in a moment.',
  timeout: 'Try again in a moment.',
  network: 'Check that the API is running.',
}

// describeError returns display text for any thrown value. Backend
// messages are client-safe and rendered as plain text.
export function describeError(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    const hint = hints[err.code]
    return hint ? `${err.message} ${hint}` : err.message
  }
  return 'Something went wrong.'
}

export function isAbort(err: unknown): boolean {
  return err instanceof DOMException && err.name === 'AbortError'
}
EOF
cat > src/composables/useDraftStream.ts <<'EOF'
import { onUnmounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { streamDrafts, type DraftField, type DraftsDone } from '../api/campaigns'
import { describeError, isAbort } from './errors'

// useDraftStream runs one draft generation at a time and exposes the text
// streamed so far for display. The stream is aborted on unmount, which
// cancels the model call on the server.
export function useDraftStream() {
  const texts = reactive<Record<DraftField, string>>({ sms_body: '', email_subject: '', email_body: '' })
  const streaming = ref(false)
  const error = ref('')
  let controller: AbortController | null = null

  function reset() {
    texts.sms_body = ''
    texts.email_subject = ''
    texts.email_body = ''
  }

  // start resolves with the stored drafts, or null on failure or abort.
  async function start(id: string): Promise<DraftsDone | null> {
    if (streaming.value) return null
    controller = new AbortController()
    streaming.value = true
    error.value = ''
    reset()
    let attempt = 0
    try {
      return await streamDrafts(
        id,
        {
          onDelta(delta) {
            // A repair attempt restarts the text; never mix attempts.
            if (delta.attempt !== attempt) {
              attempt = delta.attempt
              reset()
            }
            texts[delta.field] += delta.text
          },
        },
        controller.signal,
      )
    } catch (err) {
      if (!isAbort(err)) error.value = describeError(err)
      return null
    } finally {
      streaming.value = false
      controller = null
    }
  }

  function cancel() {
    controller

cat > src/test/helpers.ts <<'EOF'
// Test helpers: fetch stubbing, controllable streams, and API fixtures in
// the backend's wire format.
import { vi } from 'vitest'

export function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
}

export function errorResponse(status: number, code: string, error: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Response {
  return jsonResponse({ error, code, ...extra }, status)
}

// ControlledStream is a response body the test feeds chunk by chunk.
export interface ControlledStream {
  response: Response
  push: (text: string) => void
  event: (name: string, data: unknown) => void
  close: () => void
}

export function controlledStream(): ControlledStream {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder()
  let ctrl!: ReadableStreamDefaultController<Uint8Array>
  const body = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    start(c) {
      ctrl = c
    },
  })
  const push = (text: string) => ctrl.enqueue(encoder.encode(text))
  return {
    response: new Response(body, { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream' } }),
    push,
    event: (name, data) => push(`event: ${name}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`),
    close: () => ctrl.close(),
  }
}

export interface RecordedRequest {
  method: string
  url: string
  body: unknown
  signal: AbortSignal | null
}

type Handler = (req: RecordedRequest) => Response | Promise<Response>

// stubFetch routes requests by "METHOD path" and records them. Unrouted
// requests fail the test loudly.
export function stubFetch(routes: Record<string, Handler>) {
  const requests: RecordedRequest[] = []
  const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const url = typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.toString()
    const method = init?.method ?? 'GET'
    const body = typeof init?.body === 'string' ? (JSON.parse(init.body) as unknown) : undefined
    const req: RecordedRequest = { method, url, body, signal: init?.signal ?? null }
    requests.push(req)
    const handler = routes[`${method} ${url}`]
    if (!handler) throw new Error(`unexpected request ${method} ${url}`)
    return handler(req)
  })
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
  return { requests, fetchMock }
}

            city: 'İ

All 23 frontend tests pass. Next: lint and type-check, plus the Vite dev proxy, which currently strips `/api` from `/api/v1`:

**🔧 Bash** — Fix dev proxy, lint, type-check, test frontend

~~~~
sed -i 's/^export function streamOf/function streamOf/' src/api/sse.spec.ts && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='vite.config.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''    proxy: {
      '/api': {''','''    // First match wins: /api/v1 is served by the Go API under the same
    // path; other /api paths (health checks) have the prefix stripped,
    // matching nginx.conf.
    proxy: {
      '/api/v1': {
        target: process.env.API_PROXY_TARGET ?? 'http://localhost:8080',
      },
      '/api': {''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
npx eslint . --fix >/dev/null 2>&1; npm run lint 2>&1 | tail -15 && npm run typecheck 2>&1 | tail -5 && npm test 2>&1 | tail -4

C=$(curl -s -X POST "http://$FE/api/v1/campaigns" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "$B") && echo "$C" | jq -c '{id,status,version,channels,counts:.audience.counts,summary:.audience.summary}' && ID=$(echo "$C" | jq -r .id) && \
echo "--- stream (timed)" && time (curl -sN -X POST "http://$FE/api/v1/campaigns/$ID/drafts" | tee /tmp/claude-1000/-home-myubuntu-workspace-GolandProjects-smart-campaign-composer-v3/bee6a4ff-4e34-4ea6-90bf-e09b26aa41e0/scratchpad/stream.txt | grep -c '^event: delta') && tail -3 /tmp/claude-1000/-home-myubuntu-workspace-GolandProjects-smart-campaign-composer-v3/bee6a4ff-4e34-4ea6-90bf-e09b26aa41e0/scratchpad/stream.txt | cut -c1-300 && \

## 🤖 Claude

Streaming through nginx works: 16 deltas over about 0.6s, then `done`. The approval steps broke only because zsh's `echo` expands the `\n` in the JSON. Re-running those with `printf`:

**🔧 Bash** — Verify approval rejections, idempotency, and DB state

~~~~
FE=$(docker compose port frontend 8080); ID=01a168ca-0758-7b20-a14d-7689eca8cf87; G=$(curl -s "http://$FE/api/v1/campaigns/$ID"); V=$(printf '%s' "$G" | jq .version); H=$(printf '%s' "$G" | jq -r .content_hash); echo "v=$V"; \
post() { curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST "http://$FE/api/v1/campaigns/$ID/approve" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "$1"; }; \
echo "--- stale version"; post "{\"version\":$((V-1)),\"content_hash\":\"$H\",\"approved_by\":\"Deniz\"}"; \
echo "--- hash mismatch"; post "{\"version\":$V,\"content_hash\":\"$(printf 'b%.0s' $(seq 64))\",\"approved_by\":\"Deniz\"}"; \
echo "--- approve"; post "{\"version\":$V,\"content_hash\":\"$H\",\"approved_by\":\"Deniz\"}" | cut -c1-120; \
echo "--- re-approve"; post "{\"version\":$V,\"content_hash\":\"$H\",\"approved_by\":\"Deniz\"}" | grep -o ' [0-9]*$'; \
echo "--- edit after approval"; curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X PATCH "http://$FE/api/v1/campaigns/$ID" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"version\":$((V+1)),\"channels\":[\"sms\"],\"sms_body\":\"x\",\"email_subject\":\"x\",\"email_body\":\"x\"}"; \
echo "--- db"; docker compose exec -T postgres sh -c 'psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -c "SELECT status, version, approved_by, approved_at IS NOT NULL AS approved, generation_token FROM campaigns WHERE id = '"'$ID'"'" -c "SELECT action, actor, from_status, to_status, version FROM campaign_events WHERE campaign_id = '"'$ID'"' ORDER BY id" -c "\dt"'
~~~~
